end_date=end_date\n\\)\n\n# 处理PE数据\nif isinstance\\(pe_data, pd.Series\\):\n    pe_df = pe_data.to_frame\\(name='pe_ratio'\\)\nelif isinstance\\(pe_data, pd.DataFrame\\):\n    pe_df = pe_data\n    if price_data.join\\(pe_df, how='left'\\)\n\n# 3. 查询季度财务数据（净利润）\nprint\\(\"正在获取季度财务数据...\"\\)\n# 扩大时间范围以获取足够的季度数据\nfinance_start = \\(datetime.now\\(\\) - start_date=finance_start,\n    end_date=end_date,\n    frequency='quarterly'\n\\)\n\nprint\\(f\"\\\\n获取到 {len\\(price_data\\)} 个交易日数据\"\\)\nprint\\(f\"获取到 {len\\(financials\\) if financials is not None else 0} 个季度财务数据点\"\\)\n\n# 处理季度财务数据\nif financials is not None and not financials.empty:\n    financials = financials.reset_index\\(\\)\n    if 'date' in financials.columns:\n     if pd.notna\\(net_profit\\):\n            print\\(f\"{idx.strftime\\('%Y-%m-%d'\\)}: 净利润 = {net_profit/1e8:.2f} 亿元\"\\)\n    print\\(\"=\" * 80\\)\n\n# 计算涨跌幅\nfirst_close = {return_pct:+.2f}%\"\\)\nprint\\(f\"最新PE: {price_data.iloc[-1]['pe_ratio']:.2f}\" if pd.notna\\(price_data.iloc[-1]['pe_ratio']\\) else \"PE数据缺失\"\\)\n\n# 绘制增强版走势图\nplt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] =         color='#F18F01', label='市盈率\\(PE\\)', zorder=2\\)\n    ax2.fill_between\\(price_data.index, price_data['pe_ratio'], alpha=0.2, color='#F18F01'\\)\n    \n    # 标注每隔一段时间的PE值\n    sample_indices
